        d_corte = st.number_input(t('pl_corte'), 1, 30, lead.get('CORTE', 3), key='lt_corte')

        d_conf = st.number_input(t('pl_conf'), 1, 60, lead.get('CONFECAO', 8), key='lt_conf')

            for stg, dys in (('CORTE', d_corte), ('CONFECAO', d_conf), ('ACABAMENTO', d_acab)):
                execute_sql("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO planning_params (stage, days) VALUES (?, ?)", (stg, int(dys)))
            flash('success', t('ok_lead'))
            st.rerun()

    plan = plan_all_pos({'CORTE': int(d_corte), 'CONFECAO': int(d_conf), 'ACABAMENTO': int(d_acab)})
    if not plan:
        st.info(t('pl_none'))
        return

    w0 = min(min(p['corte'] for p in plan), date.today())
    w1 = max(p['entrega'] for p in plan)
    grid = 'grid-template-columns:150px 1.3fr 88px 88px 88px 88px 104px 50px 118px 1.6fr;'
    head = (f'<div style="display:grid;{grid}gap:8px;padding:8px 10px;font-size:11px;'
            f'color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;min-width:1150px;">'

            f'<div>{t("pl_c_cut")}</div><div>{t("pl_c_conf")}</div><div>{t("pl_c_fin")}</div>'
            f'<div>{t("pl_c_fab")}</div><div>{t("pl_c_slack")}</div><div>{t("pl_c_state")}</div>'
            f'<div>{t("pl_c_gantt")} <span style="font-weight:400;">({t("pl_legend")})</span></div></div>')
    rows_html = ''
    for p in plan:
        rows_html += f"""
        <div style="display:grid;{grid}gap:8px;align-items:center;padding:8px 10px;
                    border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);font-size:12px;min-width:1150px;">
            <div style="font-weight:600;">{p['po']}</div>
            <div><div>{p['modelo']}</div>
                 <div style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11px;">{p['ref']} · {p['conf']}</div></div>
            <div style="font-weight:600;">{p['entrega'].isoformat()}</div>
            <div>{p['corte'].isoformat()}</div>
            <div>{p['conf_ini'].isoformat()}</div>
            <div>{p['acab_ini'].isoformat()}</div>
            <div style="color:var(--muted);">{p['fab']}</div>
            <div>{t('pl_slack_d', n=p['slack']) if p['slack'] is not None else '—'}</div>
            <div>{p['estado']}</div>
            <div>{gantt_bar_html(p['corte'], p['conf_ini'], p['acab_ini'], p['entrega'], w0, w1)}</div>
        </div>"""
    st.markdown(f"""
    <div style="border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;overflow-x:auto;background:var(--border);">
        <div style="background:transparent;">{head}{rows_html}</div>
    </div>
    """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

    st.markdown(f'<div class="section-title">{t("ig_script")}</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)

    log = query_to_df("SELECT date_time, source, status, detail FROM sync_log ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 10")
    render_table(log, height=220)
    st.info(t('ig_manual'))

# ===================== v8.0: RESET & RECARGA DE DADOS =====================
_RESET_TABLES = ('fabric_rolls', 'incoming_fabric', 'production', 'consumptions', 'movements')

_AUDIT_ENTITY = {
    'fabrijeans / costa correia': 'Fabrijeans / Costa C',
    'tyrrell': 'Tyrrell', 'antónio & carla': 'António & Carla', 'acorfato': 'Acorfato',
    'samidel': 'Samidel', 'denimworks': 'Denimworks', 'vermis': 'Vermis',
    'xbs': 'XBS', 'riopele': 'Riopele',
}

def _map_conf_name(raw):
    """Nome NetSuite do confeccionador → nome canónico da app (CONFECCIONADORES)."""
    s = str(raw or '').strip()
    sl = s.lower()
    for k, v in (('samidel', 'Samidel'), ('costa correia', 'Costa Correia'),
                 ('tyrrel', 'Tyrrell'), ('acorfato', 'Acorfato'), ('fabrijeans', 'Fabrijeans'),
                 ('antónio & carla', 'António & Carla'), ('antonio & carla', 'António & Carla'),
                 ('denimworks', 'Denimworks'), ('vermis', 'Vermis')):
        if k in sl:
            return v
    return s

def _match_ref_prefix(item):
    """Ref do catálogo (fabric_refs) com o prefixo mais longo que casa com o texto."""
    best = None
    for r in query_to_df("SELECT ref_code FROM fabric_refs")['ref_code'].tolist():
        if item.startswith(r) and (best is None or len(r) > len(best)):
            best = r
    return best

def _split_ref_color(text):
    """'REF - Cor' → (ref, cor). Sem separador: ref = prefixo do catálogo, cor = resto."""
    s = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', str(text or '')).strip()
    if not s:
        return '', ''
    if ' - ' in s:
        ref, _, col = s.partition(' - ')
        return ref.strip(), col.strip()
    m = _match_ref_prefix(s)
    if m:
        return m, s[len(m):].strip(' -')
    parts = s.split(' ', 1)
    return parts[0], (parts[1].strip() if len(parts) > 1 else '')

def _ensure_refs(refs_desc):
    """Garante entradas no catálogo fabric_refs para refs novas dos uploads.
    Aceita refs simples ou tuplos (ref, descrição)."""
    new = []
    for item in refs_desc:
        ref, desc = item if isinstance(item, tuple) else (item, None)
        if not ref:
            continue
        execute_sql("""INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fabric_refs (ref_code, description, supplier, reorder_point, unit)
                       VALUES (?,?,?,500,'m')""", (ref, desc or None, None))
        new.append(ref)
    return sorted(set(new))

def parse_audit_stock(data):
    """Audit Excel (folha stock_CWxx) → lotes agregados por ref+cor+entidade+estado."""
    xls = pd.ExcelFile(BytesIO(data))
    sheet = next((s for s in xls.sheet_names if str(s).lower().startswith('stock')), xls.sheet_names[0])
    df = xls.parse(sheet)
    ref_col = next((c for c in df.columns if 'reference' in str(c).lower()), None)
    tot_col = next((c for c in df.columns if str(c).strip().lower() == 'total'), None)
    if not ref_col:
        raise ValueError('coluna Reference / Color não encontrada')
    ent_cols = [c for c in df.columns if c not in (ref_col, tot_col) and 'article' not in str(c).lower()]
    rows = []
    for _, r in df.iterrows():
        rc = r.get(ref_col)
        if pd.isna(rc):
            continue
        ref, color = _split_ref_color(rc)
        if not ref or ref.lower() == 'total':
            continue
        cells = []
        for c in ent_cols:
            v = pd.to_numeric(r.get(c), errors='coerce')
            if pd.isna(v) or v == 0:
                continue
            head = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', str(c)).strip()
            status = 'IN_PROCESS' if '(in process)' in head.lower() else 'AVAILABLE'
            ent = re.sub(r'\((stock|in process)\)', '', head, flags=re.I).strip()
            ent = _AUDIT_ENTITY.get(ent.lower(), ent)
            cells.append([ent, status, float(v)])
        # ajustes negativos do audit (ex.: -925,38m em processo): o Total da linha é
        # autoridade — retira-se o excesso do(s) maior(es) lote(s) positivo(s) da linha
        row_tot = pd.to_numeric(r.get(tot_col), errors='coerce') if tot_col else None
        pos_sum = sum(x[2] for x in cells if x[2] > 0)
        if any(x[2] < 0 for x in cells) and row_tot is not None and pd.notna(row_tot):
            excess = pos_sum - float(row_tot)
            adj = []
            for x in sorted((x for x in cells if x[2] > 0), key=lambda y: -y[2]):
                if excess <= 0.004:
                    break
                take = min(x[2], excess)
                x[2] -= take
                excess -= take
                adj.append(f'ajuste audit {-take:,.2f}m (célula negativa na linha)')
        else:
            adj = []
        for x in cells:
            if x[2] <= 0:
                continue
            rows.append({'ref': ref, 'color': color, 'entity': x[0], 'status': x[1],
                         'metres': round(x[2], 2), 'adj': '; '.join(adj) if adj else None})
    # total declarado no ficheiro: linha de grand-total (sem ref) ou soma da coluna Total dos dados
    file_tot = None
    if tot_col:
        tot_rows = pd.to_numeric(df.loc[df[ref_col].isna(), tot_col], errors='coerce').dropna()
        if len(tot_rows):
            file_tot = float(tot_rows.max())
        else:
            file_tot = float(pd.to_numeric(df[tot_col], errors='coerce').sum())
    return rows, file_tot, sheet

def parse_fabric_pos(data):
    """PurchaseOrders NetSuite (tecido) → uma linha por PO · ref · cor."""
    df = pd.read_excel(BytesIO(data))
    df = df[df['Document Number'].astype(str).str.startswith('PO')].copy()
    if df.empty:
        return [], [], 0
    rc = df['Item Number'].astype(str).apply(_split_ref_color)
    df['ref'] = [x[0] for x in rc]
    df['color'] = [x[1] for x in rc]
    rows = []
    for (po, ref, color), g in df.groupby(['Document Number', 'ref', 'color'], dropna=False):
        qty = float(pd.to_numeric(g['Quantity'], errors='coerce').sum())
        due = pd.to_datetime(g['Due Date/Receive By'].iloc[0], errors='coerce')
        cre = pd.to_datetime(g['Creation Date'].iloc[0], errors='coerce')
        rows.append({
            'po': str(po), 'ref': ref, 'color': color,
            'supplier': str(g['Name'].iloc[0]).strip(), 'metres': round(qty, 1),
            'expected': due.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') if pd.notna(due) else None,
            'created': cre.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') if pd.notna(cre) else None,
            'memo': str(g['Memo (Main)'].iloc[0] or '').strip(),
        })
    counts = {}
    for r in rows:
        counts[r['po']] = counts.get(r['po'], 0) + 1
    for r in rows:
        r['po_key'] = r['po'] if counts[r['po']] == 1 else f"{r['po']}·{r['color'] or r['ref']}"
    existing = set(query_to_df("SELECT ref_code FROM fabric_refs")['ref_code'])
    new_refs = sorted({r['ref'] for r in rows if r['ref']} - existing)
    return rows, new_refs, len(counts)

def parse_garment_pos(data):
    """PurchaseOrders NetSuite (garment) → POs finais sem ref de tecido."""
    df = pd.read_excel(BytesIO(data))
    df = df[df['Document Number'].astype(str).str.startswith('PO')].copy()
    rows = []
    seen = set()
    for _, r in df.iterrows():
        po = str(r['Document Number']).strip()
        if po in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(po)
        due = pd.to_datetime(r['Due Date/Receive By'], errors='coerce')
        cre = pd.to_datetime(r['Date'], errors='coerce')
        rows.append({
            'po': po, 'model': str(r['Memo (Main)'] or '').strip(),
            'conf': _map_conf_name(r['Company Name']),
            'qty': int(pd.to_numeric(r['Qty Order'], errors='coerce') or 0),
            'expected': due.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') if pd.notna(due) else None,
            'created': cre.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') if pd.notna(cre) else None,
        })
    return rows

def parse_history(data):
    """All PO.xlsx (sheets por ano) → consumos históricos + info de PO para backfill."""
    xls = pd.ExcelFile(BytesIO(data))
    rows, dupes = [], 0
    seen = set()
    for sh in xls.sheet_names:
        if not re.fullmatch(r'(19|20)\d{2}', str(sh).strip()):
            continue
        year = int(sh)
        df = xls.parse(sh)
        cols = {str(c).strip().lower(): c for c in df.columns}
        def pick(*names):
            return next((cols[n] for n in names if n in cols), None)
        c_week, c_ref = pick('production week'), pick('fabric ref')
        c_mfr, c_po, c_memo = pick('manufacturer'), pick('po'), pick('memo')
        c_used = pick('meters used for po')
        c_est = pick('estimated consumption (mt)', 'estimated consumption')
        c_cut = pick('qty cut', 'qty cut´s')
        c_qp = pick('qty po', 'po´s qty', 'qty po´s')
        if not all([c_po, c_used]):
            continue
        for _, r in df.iterrows():
            po = str(r.get(c_po) or '').strip()
            if not po.startswith('POAPS'):
                continue
            used = pd.to_numeric(r.get(c_used), errors='coerce')
            if pd.isna(used) or used <= 0:
                continue
            est = pd.to_numeric(r.get(c_est), errors='coerce') if c_est else None
            est = float(est) if est is not None and pd.notna(est) and est > 0 else None
            cut = pd.to_numeric(r.get(c_cut), errors='coerce') if c_cut else None
            cut = int(cut) if cut is not None and pd.notna(cut) else None
            qp = pd.to_numeric(r.get(c_qp), errors='coerce') if c_qp else None
            qp = int(qp) if qp is not None and pd.notna(qp) else (cut or 0)
            memo = str(r.get(c_memo) or '').strip()
            key = (po, memo, float(used), cut)
            if key in seen:
                dupes += 1
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            ref, color = _split_ref_color(r.get(c_ref))
            try:
                wk = int(float(r.get(c_week)))
                d_cut = datetime.fromisocalendar(year, min(max(wk, 1), 53), 1).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
            except Exception:
                d_cut = f'{year}-01-01'
            dev = round((float(used) - est) / est * 100, 1) if est else None
            rows.append({
                'po': po, 'model': memo, 'conf': _map_conf_name(r.get(c_mfr)),
                'ref': ref, 'color': color, 'pcs': cut, 'qty_po': qp,
                'est': est, 'used': round(float(used), 2), 'dev': dev,
                'date': d_cut, 'year': year,
            })
    return rows, dupes

def apply_reset_data():
    """Apaga os dados operacionais. Preserva fabric_refs, mapas de consumo (standards),
    planning_params, integration_config e sync_log (onde regista o reset)."""
    counts = {tb: query_to_df(f"SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM {tb}")['n'].iloc[0] for tb in _RESET_TABLES}
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cur = conn.cursor()
    for tb in _RESET_TABLES:
        cur.execute(f"DELETE FROM {tb}")
    detail = '; '.join(f'{tb}={counts[tb]}' for tb in _RESET_TABLES)
    cur.execute("INSERT INTO sync_log (date_time, source, status, detail) VALUES (?,?,?,?)",
                (datetime.now().isoformat(), 'RESET', 'ok', f'Reset dados operacionais: {detail}'))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return counts

def apply_stock_upload(rows, sheet):
    """Lotes agregados do audit: 1 lote AVAILABLE e/ou 1 IN_PROCESS por ref+cor+entidade."""
    existing = set(query_to_df("SELECT ref_code FROM fabric_refs")['ref_code'])
    new_refs = _ensure_refs(sorted({r['ref'] for r in rows} - existing))
    mx = 0
    for tk in query_to_df("SELECT token FROM fabric_rolls WHERE token LIKE 'AGG-%'")['token'].tolist():
        m = re.search(r'AGG-(\d+)', tk)
        if m:
            mx = max(mx, int(m.group(1)))
    now = datetime.now().isoformat()
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cur = conn.cursor()
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        tok = f'AGG-{mx + i + 1:04d}'
        notes = f'Lote agregado — upload stock ({sheet})'
        if r.get('adj'):
            notes += f' · {r["adj"]}'
        cur.execute("INSERT INTO fabric_rolls VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                    (tok, r['ref'], r['metres'], f'Upload {sheet}', r['color'], r['entity'],
                     r['status'], None, now, now, notes))
        cur.execute("""INSERT INTO movements (date_time, move_type, token, from_location, to_location,
                       ref_code, color, metres, po_garment, notes)
                       VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
                    (now, 'RECEIPT', tok, None, r['entity'], r['ref'], r['color'], r['metres'], None,
                     f'Upload stock agregado ({sheet}) — {r["status"]}'))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return len(rows), new_refs

def apply_fabric_po_upload(rows):
    """Encomendas de tecido → incoming_fabric (EXPECTED, destino atribuído depois na grelha)."""
    existing = set(query_to_df("SELECT ref_code FROM fabric_refs")['ref_code'])
    new_refs = _ensure_refs(sorted({(r['ref'], r['memo']) for r in rows if r['ref'] not in existing}))
    now = datetime.now().isoformat()
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cur = conn.cursor()
    for r in rows:
        cur.execute("""INSERT OR REPLACE INTO incoming_fabric
                       (po_number, supplier, ref_code, total_metres, expected_date, status,
                        tracking_ref, date_created, color, destination, date_invoiced)
                       VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
                    (r['po_key'], r['supplier'], r['ref'], r['metres'], r['expected'], 'EXPECTED',
                     None, r['created'] or now, r['color'], None, None))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return new_refs

def apply_garment_po_upload(rows):
    """POs garment finais → production (PENDING, fabric_ref NULL — completar na tabela)."""
    now = datetime.now().isoformat()
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cur = conn.cursor()
    for r in rows:
        cur.execute("""INSERT OR REPLACE INTO production
                       (po_number, model_name, confeccionador, po_qty, fabric_ref, metres_expected,
                        expected_date, status, date_created, base_model, color, date_invoiced)
                       VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
                    (r['po'], r['model'], r['conf'], r['qty'], None, None,
                     r['expected'], 'PENDING', r['created'] or now, None, None, None))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return len(rows)

def apply_history_upload(rows):
    """Histórico de consumos: insere consumos validados, cria POs antigas (INVOICED) para
    ligar ref/molde, atualiza refs em falta e recalcula o real médio — standards intactos."""
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cur = conn.cursor()
    ins_c = 0
    for r in rows:
        cur.execute("""INSERT INTO consumptions (po_garment, model_name, pcs_cut, metres_expected,
                       metres_actual, deviation_pct, date_cut, confeccionador, notes,
                       authorized, authorized_note)
                       VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1,'Importação histórica validada')""",
                    (r['po'], r['model'], r['pcs'], r['est'], r['used'], r['dev'], r['date'],
                     r['conf'], f'Histórico importado (All PO {r["year"]})'))
        ins_c += 1
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    cre_p = upd_p = 0
    by_po = {}
    for r in rows:
        by_po.setdefault(r['po'], r)
    for po, r in by_po.items():
        ex = query_to_df("SELECT fabric_ref FROM production WHERE po_number = ?", (po,))
        if ex.empty:
            execute_sql("""INSERT INTO production (po_number, model_name, confeccionador, po_qty,
                           fabric_ref, metres_expected, expected_date, status, date_created,
                           base_model, color, date_invoiced)
                           VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
                        (po, r['model'], r['conf'], r['qty_po'], r['ref'], r['est'], None,
                         'INVOICED', r['date'], None, r['color'], r['date']))
            cre_p += 1
        elif not ex.iloc[0]['fabric_ref'] and r['ref']:
            execute_sql("UPDATE production SET fabric_ref = ?, color = COALESCE(color, ?) WHERE po_number = ?",
                        (r['ref'], r['color'], po))
            upd_p += 1
    # refs novas do histórico entram no catálogo
    existing = set(query_to_df("SELECT ref_code FROM fabric_refs")['ref_code'])
    _ensure_refs(sorted({r['ref'] for r in rows if r['ref']} - existing))
    # recalcular mapas — INSERT OR IGNORE / só m_per_pc_actual: standards preservados
    execute_sql("""
        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO consumption_map (model_name, fabric_ref, m_per_pc_expected, m_per_pc_actual)
        SELECT c.model_name, p.fabric_ref,
               ROUND(AVG(c.metres_expected / CAST(c.pcs_cut AS REAL)), 2),
               ROUND(AVG(c.metres_actual / CAST(c.pcs_cut AS REAL)), 3)
        FROM consumptions c JOIN production p ON c.po_garment = p.po_number
        WHERE c.pcs_cut > 0 AND c.metres_expected > 0 AND p.fabric_ref IS NOT NULL
        GROUP BY c.model_name, p.fabric_ref""")
    execute_sql("""
        UPDATE consumption_map SET m_per_pc_actual = (
            SELECT ROUND(AVG(c.metres_actual / CAST(c.pcs_cut AS REAL)), 3)
            FROM consumptions c
            WHERE c.pcs_cut > 0 AND (
                c.model_name = consumption_map.model_name
                OR c.model_name LIKE '%' || REPLACE(consumption_map.model_name, ' Men Plain', '') || '%'))""")
    for e in query_to_df("SELECT base_model, fit, fabric_ref FROM consumption_map_v4").to_dict('records'):
        update_map_actual(e['base_model'], e['fit'], e['fabric_ref'])
    # modelos do histórico sem molde base no mapa → avisar o utilizador
    warn, seen_w = [], set()
    for r in rows:
        k = (r['model'], r['ref'])
        if k in seen_w or not r['ref'] or not r['model']:
            continue
        seen_w.add(k)
        if not resolve_map_entry(r['model'], r['ref'], None):
            warn.append(f"{r['model']} · {r['ref']}")
    return ins_c, cre_p, upd_p, sorted(set(warn))

def render_reset_reload():
    """🔄 Reset & Recarga (v8) — backup, limpeza segura e 4 uploads oficiais."""
    st.markdown(f"""
    <div class="main-header">
        <div>
            <h1>{t('rs_title')}</h1>
            <p>{t('rs_sub')}</p>
        </div>
    </div>
    """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

    # --- 1) backup -----------------------------------------------------------
    st.markdown(f'<div class="section-title">{t("rs_backup_t")}</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    st.markdown(f'<div class="section-subtitle">{t("rs_backup_d")}</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    try:
        with open(DB_PATH, 'rb') as _f:
            _db_bytes = _f.read()
        st.download_button(t('rs_backup_btn'), _db_bytes,
                           f'snt_cmt_backup_{datetime.now():%Y%m%d_%H%M}.db',
                           'application/octet-stream', key='rs_backup')
    except Exception as e:
        st.error(str(e))

    # --- 2) reset ------------------------------------------------------------
    st.markdown(f'<div class="section-title">{t("rs_wipe_t")}</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    st.markdown(f'<div class="section-subtitle">{t("rs_wipe_d")}</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    _conf = st.text_input(t('rs_confirm_lbl'), key='rs_confirm')
    if st.button(t('rs_wipe_btn'), key='rs_wipe'):
        if _conf.strip() == 'RESET':
            counts = apply_reset_data()
            flash('success', t('ok_wipe', r=counts['fabric_rolls'], i=counts['incoming_fabric'],
                               p=counts['production'], c=counts['consumptions'], m=counts['movements']))
            st.rerun()
        else:
            st.error(t('rs_wipe_err'))

    # --- 3) uploads ----------------------------------------------------------
    st.markdown(f'<div class="section-title">{t("rs_up_t")}</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)

    def _already(tag, f):
        return st.session_state.get(f'_rs_done_{tag}') == (f.name, f.size)
    def _mark_done(tag, f):
        st.session_state[f'_rs_done_{tag}'] = (f.name, f.size)

    # a) stock agregado
    with st.expander(t('rs_up_stock'), expanded=True):
        st.caption(t('rs_up_stock_d'))
        f = st.file_uploader('audit', type=['xlsx', 'xls'], key='rs_f_stock', label_visibility='collapsed')
        if f is not None:
            try:
                rows, file_tot, sheet = parse_audit_stock(f.getvalue())
                if not rows:
                    st.warning(t('rs_nothing'))
                else:
                    tot = sum(r['metres'] for r in rows)
                    av = sum(r['metres'] for r in rows if r['status'] == 'AVAILABLE')
                    st.caption(t('rs_recon', f=f"{file_tot:,.1f}" if file_tot else '—', i=f"{tot:,.1f}"))
                    st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(rows).head(25), use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
                    if _already('stock', f):
                        st.caption(t('rs_loaded'))
                    elif st.button(f"{t('rs_load')} ({len(rows)})", key='rs_b_stock'):
                        n, new_refs = apply_stock_upload(rows, sheet)
                        _mark_done('stock', f)
                        flash('success', t('ok_up_stock', n=n, m=f"{tot:,.1f}", a=f"{av:,.1f}",
                                           p=f"{tot - av:,.1f}", r=len(new_refs)))
                        st.rerun()
            except Exception as e:
                st.error(t('rs_file_err', e=e))

    # b) tecido a chegar
    with st.expander(t('rs_up_fab')):
        st.caption(t('rs_up_fab_d'))
        f = st.file_uploader('fabpo', type=['xlsx', 'xls'], key='rs_f_fab', label_visibility='collapsed')
        if f is not None:
            try:
                rows, new_refs, n_pos = parse_fabric_pos(f.getvalue())
                if not rows:
                    st.warning(t('rs_nothing'))
                else:
                    st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(rows)[['po_key', 'supplier', 'ref', 'color', 'metres', 'expected']].head(25),
                                 use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
                    if _already('fab', f):
                        st.caption(t('rs_loaded'))
                    elif st.button(f"{t('rs_load')} ({len(rows)})", key='rs_b_fab'):
                        new_refs = apply_fabric_po_upload(rows)
                        _mark_done('fab', f)
                        flash('success', t('ok_up_fab', n=len(rows), p=n_pos, r=len(new_refs)))
                        st.rerun()
            except Exception as e:
                st.error(t('rs_file_err', e=e))

    # c) POs garment
    with st.expander(t('rs_up_garm')):
        st.caption(t('rs_up_garm_d'))
        f = st.file_uploader('garpo', type=['xlsx', 'xls'], key='rs_f_garm', label_visibility='collapsed')
        if f is not None:
            try:
                rows = parse_garment_pos(f.getvalue())
                if not rows:
                    st.warning(t('rs_nothing'))
                else:
                    st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(rows).head(25), use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
                    if _already('garm', f):
                        st.caption(t('rs_loaded'))
                    elif st.button(f"{t('rs_load')} ({len(rows)})", key='rs_b_garm'):
                        n = apply_garment_po_upload(rows)
                        _mark_done('garm', f)
                        flash('success', t('ok_up_garm', n=n))
                        st.rerun()
            except Exception as e:
                st.error(t('rs_file_err', e=e))

    # d) histórico de consumos
    with st.expander(t('rs_up_hist')):
        st.caption(t('rs_up_hist_d'))
        f = st.file_uploader('hist', type=['xlsx', 'xls'], key='rs_f_hist', label_visibility='collapsed')
        if f is not None:
            try:
                rows, dupes = parse_history(f.getvalue())
                if not rows:
                    st.warning(t('rs_nothing'))
                else:
                    tot_m = sum(r['used'] for r in rows)
                    st.caption(f"{len(rows)} consumos · {tot_m:,.1f}m · {len({r['po'] for r in rows})} POs · {dupes} dup.")
                    st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(rows)[['po', 'model', 'ref', 'color', 'pcs', 'used', 'est', 'dev', 'date', 'conf']].head(25),
                                 use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
                    if _already('hist', f):
                        st.caption(t('rs_loaded'))
                    elif st.button(f"{t('rs_load')} ({len(rows)})", key='rs_b_hist'):
                        ins_c, cre_p, upd_p, warn = apply_history_upload(rows)
                        _mark_done('hist', f)
                        st.session_state['_rs_warn_models'] = warn
                        flash('success', t('ok_up_hist', c=ins_c, d=dupes, p=cre_p, u=upd_p))
                        st.rerun()
            except Exception as e:
                st.error(t('rs_file_err', e=e))
        _warn = st.session_state.get('_rs_warn_models')
        if _warn:
            st.warning(t('rs_mould_warn', n=len(_warn)) + '  \n' + ' · '.join(_warn[:40])
                       + (' …' if len(_warn) > 40 else ''))

def render_tools():
    """⚙️ Sistema (v7) — Movimentar · Rastreio · Exportar · Integrações num único menu."""
    tool = st.radio(t('m_system'), [t('m_movement'), t('tr_title'), t('ex_title'), t('m_intg'), t('rs_title')],
                    horizontal=True, key='tools_sel', label_visibility='collapsed')
    st.markdown("<div style='margin-bottom:18px;'></div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)
    if tool == t('m_movement'):
        render_movement()
    elif tool == t('tr_title'):
        render_trace()
    elif tool == t('ex_title'):
        render_export()
    elif tool == t('rs_title'):
        render_reset_reload()
    else:
        render_integrations()


# ===================== MAIN =====================
def main():
    st.sidebar.markdown(f"""
    <div style="padding:16px 0 24px 0;text-align:center;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);margin-bottom:16px;">
        <div style="font-size:28px;margin-bottom:4px;">🏭</div>
        <div style="color:var(--text);font-size:16px;font-weight:700;">SNT APS Portugal</div>
        <div style="color:var(--faint);font-size:11px;">{t('sb_system')}</div>
        <div style="margin-top:8px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;background:rgba(34,197,94,0.1);color:#22c55e;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:12px;font-size:10px;font-weight:600;">
            <span style="width:5px;height:5px;background:#22c55e;border-radius:50%;display:inline-block;"></span> LIVE
        </div>
    </div>
    """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

    # Seletores de idioma e tema
    lc1, lc2 = st.sidebar.columns(2)
    with lc1:
        st.selectbox(t('sb_lang'), ['pt', 'en'],
                     format_func=lambda x: '🇵🇹 PT' if x == 'pt' else '🇬🇧 EN',
                     key='lang', label_visibility='collapsed')
    with lc2:
        st.selectbox(t('sb_theme'), ['dark', 'clean'],
                     format_func=lambda x: '🌙 Dark' if x == 'dark' else '☀️ Clean',
                     key='theme', label_visibility='collapsed')

    # v7: automações de arranque — cor da PO e molde auto-aplicados (1× por sessão)
    if not st.session_state.get('_v7_auto'):
        st.session_state['_v7_auto'] = True
        try:
            _nc, _nm = auto_fix_po_colors(), auto_link_models()
            if _nc or _nm:
                flash('info', t('ok_auto_v7', c=_nc, m=_nm))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # v7: 6 menus (antes 10) — badge com alertas críticos no ⚡ Hoje
    n_badge = sum(1 for a in compute_alerts() if a['sev'] == 'critical')
    today_lbl = t('m_today') + (f" ({n_badge})" if n_badge else '')
    tabs = {
        today_lbl: render_today,
        t('m_stock'): render_stock,
        t('m_production'): render_production,
        t('m_consumos'): render_consumos,
        t('m_planning'): render_planning,
        t('m_system'): render_tools,
    }

    selection = st.sidebar.radio(t('sb_nav'), list(tabs.keys()), label_visibility="collapsed")

    _fn = datetime.now()
    st.sidebar.markdown(f"""
    <div style="position:fixed;bottom:20px;left:20px;right:20px;">
        <div style="border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding-top:12px;color:var(--faint);font-size:11px;text-align:center;">
            {_fn.strftime('%d-%m-%Y')} · CW{_fn.isocalendar()[1]:02d} {_fn.year}<br>
            <span style="color:var(--accent);font-weight:600;">SNT APS Portugal</span> · {t('sb_system')}
        </div>
    </div>
    """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

    show_flash()  # v5.5: confirmações persistentes no topo da página
    tabs[selection]()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
